// @public (undocumented)
export type ListBlock = z.infer<typeof ListBlockSchema>;

// @public (undocumented)
export const ListBlockSchema: z.ZodReadonly<z.ZodObject<{
    id: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
    extensions: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodReadonly<z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodJSONSchema>>>;
    type: z.ZodLiteral<"list">;
    style: z.ZodEnum<{
        ordered: "ordered";
        unordered: "unordered";
    }>;
    items: z.ZodReadonly<z.ZodArray<z.ZodString>>;
}, z.core.$strict>>;

export class UnsupportedPageDocumentVersionError extends Error {
    constructor(receivedVersion: number);
    // (undocumented)
    readonly code = "PAGE_DOCUMENT_SCHEMA_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED";
    // (undocumented)
    readonly name = "UnsupportedPageDocumentVersionError";
    // (undocumented)
    readonly receivedVersion: number;
}

// @public (undocumented)
class UnverifiedReleasePointerError extends ReleaseContractError {
    constructor(releaseId: string);
    // (undocumented)
    readonly name: string;
    // (undocumented)
    readonly releaseId: string;
}
